We also show that isolating IEEE 802.1AS/gPTP traffic on a dedicated high-priority radio bearer reduces clock-servo instability, although endpoints carry- ing lower-priority data still experience elevated synchronisation delay under saturation because of reduced MAC scheduling frequency. Finally, the evaluated sub-6 GHz, 30 kHz-SCS con- figuration exhibits an effective latency floor of approximately 2.25 ms, indicating that sub-3 ms TSN deadlines may require radio-configuration changes such as configured grants or higher numerology

</details>


<div id='eess.SP'></div>

# eess.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [102] [Energy Efficient Multi-User Beamforming and 3D Position Optimization for SIM-Assisted UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13765)
*Chandan Kumar Sheemar,Giovanni Iacovelli,Sourabh Solanki,Wali Ullah Khan,George C. Alexandropoulos,Symeon Chatzinotas*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于堆叠智能超表面（SIM）的UAV通信系统下行多用户传输能效优化问题。通过联合优化数字预编码器、SIM各层相移及UAV三维位置，提出了一种基于变换的交替优化框架，显著提升了能效性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着无人机通信系统的快速发展，如何在硬件复杂度受限的情况下实现高效能量利用成为关键挑战。堆叠智能超表面（SIM）能够通过多层级联超表面实现波域模拟波束成形，在有限射频链路的条件下灵活操控电磁波，特别适合能量受限的空中平台。因此，需要研究SIM-UAV系统的能效最大化问题，并揭示关键设计权衡。

Method: 1. 问题建模：建立硬件感知能效最大化问题，联合优化数字预编码器、所有SIM层的相移以及UAV三维位置；2. 约束条件：包括发射功率约束、SIM运行约束和UAV部署约束；3. 算法设计：提出基于变换的交替优化框架，结合Dinkelbach方法、对偶变换和二次变换技术；4. 具体实现：闭式数字波束成形、黎曼流形优化处理SIM相移、连续凸逼近（SCA）处理UAV定位；5. 理论分析：提供收敛性和复杂度分析。

Result: 数值结果表明，提出的联合设计方案相比全数字和最大比传输基准方案显著提升了能效性能。研究还揭示了发射功率、SIM尺寸及其堆叠层数之间的重要设计权衡关系。所提算法具有良好的收敛性和可接受的计算复杂度。

Conclusion: 本文针对装备SIM的UAV通信系统，首次提出了能效最大化的联合优化框架。通过巧妙的算法设计解决了高度非凸问题，实现了硬件复杂度与能效性能的良好平衡。研究为能量受限的空中平台的SIM-UAV系统设计提供了重要理论指导，并明确了关键参数的设计权衡关系。

Abstract: This paper studies energy-efficient downlink multi-user transmissions with unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) communication systems equipped with stacked intelligent metasurfaces (SIM), enabling wave-domain analog beamforming through multiple cascaded metasurface layers, while low-dimensional digital precoding is carried out using a limited number of transmit radio-frequency chains. This architecture enables flexible electromagnetic wave manipulation with reduced hardware complexity, making it particularly suitable for energy-constrained aerial platforms. We formulate a hardware-aware energy-efficiency (EE) maximization problem aiming to jointly optimize the digital precoder, the phase shifts of all SIM layers, and the three-dimensional UAV position under transmit-power, SIM operation, and UAV deployment constraints. The resulting problem is highly non-convex due to the fractional objective, the cascaded SIM structure and the unit-modulus phase constraints of the constituent metasurface layers, as well as the non-linear UAV-dependent channel. To address these challenges, we develop a transform-based alternating optimization framework that combines Dinkelbach's method, dual and quadratic transforms, to enable closed-form digital beamforming, Riemannian manifold optimization for SIM phase shifts, and successive convex approximation (SCA) for UAV positioning. Convergence and complexity analyses are provided to characterize the proposed algorithm. The presented numerical results showcase that the proposed joint design significantly improves EE compared with fully digital and maximum ratio transmission benchmark schemes, while revealing important design trade-offs among transmit power, SIM size, and the number of its constituent stacked layers.

</details>


### [103] [Compact Microstrip Triplexer Using Square Open-Loop Resonators with High Isolation for 5G Sub-6 GHz Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13804)
*Bhaskarareddy N C,Rashmi Ravi,Augustine O. Nwajana*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文设计了一种适用于5G sub-6 GHz应用的紧凑型微带三工器，工作频率为2.2 GHz、2.6 GHz和3.0 GHz。采用方形开环谐振器构成三个独立的三阶切比雪夫带通滤波器通道，通过T型接头实现公共输入端口集成。仿真结果表明，三个通道的隔离度均超过45 dB，显著优于近期多数微带三工器设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着多标准5G New Radio sub-6 GHz系统的快速发展，市场急需紧凑、高隔离度的复用器件，能够在单一共享天线端口上同时路由多个频段通道，且无跨频段干扰。

Method: 提出紧凑型微带三工器设计，采用方形开环谐振器构建三个独立三阶切比雪夫带通滤波器通道（共九个谐振极点）。每个通道由标准归一化切比雪夫低通原型综合而成。三个通道通过T型接头在公共输入端口集成，并采用适当尺寸的传输线截头实现高通道间隔离。设计基于全波电磁仿真验证。

Result: 全波电磁仿真结果显示：三个通道的回波损耗分别为21.1 dB、23.1 dB和22.8 dB；插损分别为1.08 dB、1.01 dB和0.98 dB；通道间隔离度分别为45.7 dB、45.2 dB和45.7 dB。

Conclusion: 实现的通道间隔离度超过45 dB，相比近期大多数已报道的微带三工器设计有显著提升，满足了5G sub-6 GHz应用对紧凑型高隔离复用器件的迫切需求。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of multi-standard 5G New Radio sub-6 GHz systems have intensified the demand for compact, high-isolation multiplexing components capable of simultaneously routing multiple frequency channels through a single shared antenna port without cross-band interference. This paper presents the design and full-wave electromagnetic simulation of a compact microstrip triplexer operating at 2.2, 2.6, and 3.0 GHz for 5G sub-6 GHz applications. The proposed triplexer employs square open-loop resonators arranged as three independent three-pole Chebyshev bandpass filter channels, yielding nine resonator poles in total. Each channel is synthesised from the standard normalised Chebyshev lowpass prototype. The three channels are integrated at a common input port via a T-junction, with connecting transmission line stubs dimensioned to enforce high inter-channel isolation. Full-wave EM simulation results demonstrate return losses of 21.1 dB, 23.1 dB, and 22.8 dB; insertion losses of 1.08 dB, 1.01 dB, and 0.98 dB; and inter-channel isolations of 45.7 dB, 45.2 dB, and 45.7 dB, respectively. The achieved inter-channel isolation exceeding 45 dB represents a significant improvement over the majority of recently reported microstrip triplexer designs.

</details>


### [104] [Dual-Layer Over-the-Air Federated Learning in LEO Satellite Networks: Architecture, Key Technologies and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13885)
*Zhendong Li,Shaojie Wang,Zhou Su,Tom H. Luan,Ruijin Sun,Ying Wang,Wen Chen*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于低地球轨道(LEO)卫星网络的新型边缘智能框架，将空中计算(OTA)与自适应波束跳变(BH)相结合，为联邦学习在物联网部署中的应用提供了创新解决方案，有效克服了卫星平台的带宽和功率限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LEO卫星网络成为全球边缘智能基础设施，需要在有限带宽和功率约束下扩展联邦学习至广泛的物联网部署。传统方法难以同时满足资源效率和实时性要求，亟需创新框架实现物理层聚合与动态资源编排的无缝融合。

Method: 研究提出了双层OTA模型和自适应波束跳变机制：(1)建立双层OTA计算框架实现物理层模拟聚合；(2)设计针对时变拓扑的自适应BH机制；(3)通过调度策略优化实现空间-时间资源效率最大化；(4)建立数据新鲜度与信道质量的平衡机制。

Result: 实验结果显示：(1)模型收敛速度显著提升；(2)数据利用率大幅改善；(3)聚合时延与设备密度解耦；(4)在多种垂直行业应用场景中均表现出优越性能；(5)提出了有效的卫星FL系统实现方案。

Conclusion: 所提出的OTA-BH融合框架为卫星联邦学习系统提供了实质性性能增益，在模型收敛和数据利用方面表现突出。虽然面临实现挑战，但该框架为普遍非地面智能的演进提供了重要见解，未来研究应聚焦于实际部署优化和标准化工作。

Abstract: Low Earth orbit (LEO) satellite networks are emerging as a pivotal infrastructure for global edge intelligence. In this context, integrating over-the-air (OTA) computation with adaptive beam hopping (BH) provides an innovative framework that seamlessly merges physical-layer analog aggregation with dynamic resource orchestration. This effectively overcomes the stringent bandwidth and power constraints of space platforms while extending federated learning (FL) to pervasive Internet-of-things (IoT) deployments. In this article, we first outline the fundamental principles of the dual-layer OTA model and introduce the adaptive BH mechanism designed for time-varying topologies. Then, we summarize the distinct advantages of this learning-centric architecture, which include decoupling aggregation latency from device density, optimizing spatio-temporal resource efficiency, and balancing data freshness with channel quality. Several application scenarios are explored to highlight the framework's potential across diverse vertical industries. Furthermore, a specific case is studied to demonstrate the practical efficacy of the proposed scheduling policy. The results reveal substantial performance gains in terms of model convergence speed and data utilization for satellite-based FL systems. Finally, we discuss the implementation challenges and outline future research directions, aiming to provide insights for the evolution of ubiquitous non-terrestrial intelligence.

</details>


### [105] [Omni-Photonic Base Station: A Three-Functional-Domain Photonic Architecture for Evolutionary 6G Wireless Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13909)
*Dapeng Wang,Xiaoxiong Song,Ziling Fu,Wenyang Cheng,Jiayao Wang,Xiaogang Yan,Ze Wang,Min Zhang,Jingdi Liu,Nan Li*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出全光子基站（Omni-PBS）架构，通过光子技术在基带处理、前传传输和射频前端三个领域实现协同设计，以满足6G通信对高数据率、大规模连接以及感算智能的复合需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G移动通信对更高数据率和大规模连接提出沉浸式通信需求，同时新兴的集成感知-计算-智能场景要求基站并发提升计算能力、保证服务延迟并实现感知功能。传统电子架构在带宽、延迟和能效方面面临瓶颈，难以满足这些复合需求。

Method: 提出全光子基站演进架构，将光子技术引入三个功能域：1）光基带计算域——使用光子加速器执行线性计算密集型任务，提升AI推理能效；2）模拟光前传域——采用模拟光载无线技术替代数字前传，消除远程单元中的ADCs/DACs和数字中频芯片，降低前传带宽需求；3）微波光子射频域——利用光学真延迟波束成形、可编程光子滤波和光学外差频率转换突破电子射频前端的带宽和频率限制。通过四大协同设计原则实现系统级增益：端到端光域连续性、跨域协同设计、光计算资源调度、功能分割联合优化。

Result: 系统级增益超越单一领域之和：光子加速器使AI推理能效提高1-2个数量级；模拟光前传显著降低前传带宽需求；微波光子射频突破电子前端带宽和频率限制。整体架构有效满足6G场景的复合需求。

Conclusion: 全光子基站通过利用光子技术的超宽带宽、超低传播延迟和原生并行性等物理优势，超越电子瓶颈，为6G通信及感算智能场景提供了高性能、低功耗的解决方案，实现了跨域协同的系统级优化。

Abstract: 6G mobile communications impose immersive communication demands for higher data rates and massive connectivity, while emerging integrated sensing-computing-intelligence scenarios require base stations to concurrently enhance computing capability, guarantee service latency, and realize sensing. We propose the Omni-Photonic Base Station-a progressive evolutionary architecture that introduces photonic technologies into three functional domains, namely baseband processing, fronthaul transmission, and the RF front-end, and obtains system-level gains through cross-domain co-design. By introducing photonics, Omni-PBS harnesses the inherent physical advantages of ultra-broad bandwidth, ultra-low propagation latency, and native parallelism to transcend the aforementioned electronic bottlenecks and fulfill the compound demands of 6G scenarios. The optical baseband computing domain employs photonic accelerators to perform linear computation-intensive tasks, improving the energy efficiency of AI inference by one to two orders of magnitude. The analog optical fronthaul domain replaces digital fronthaul with analog radio-over-fiber , which eliminates the ADCs/DACs and digital intermediate-frequency chips in the remote unit and substantially reduces fronthaul bandwidth requirements. The microwave-photonic RF domain breaks through the bandwidth and frequency limitations of electronic RF front-ends via optical true-time-delay beamforming, programmable photonic filtering, and optical heterodyne frequency conversion. The three domains yield system-level gains beyond single-domain summation through four co-design principles: end-to-end optical-domain continuity, cross-domain co-design, optical computing resource scheduling, and joint optimization of functional splitting.

</details>


### [106] [Conjugate Equivariant Neural Network for Precoder Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13941)
*Shiyong Chen,Mingyu Deng,Shengqian Han*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究共轭等变性(CE)在DNN设计中的应用，提出了一种通用的共轭等变神经网络(CENN)框架用于预编码学习。证明了最优预编码策略满足CE特性，并开发了一种非线性构造方法，能够在不增加可学习参数的情况下将CE集成到现有等变网络中。仿真结果表明，CE增强网络在完全数字和RIS辅助预编码场景中都能提升学习和泛化性能，同时减少训练样本需求和训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用无线网络策略的数学性质改进DNN设计，可以提升学习性能、泛化能力并降低训练复杂度。虽然置换等变性和置换不变性已被纳入DNN架构，但共轭等变性(CE)这一重要性质尚未被充分研究。研究CE的动机在于：对于统一的预编码问题类别，当信道矩阵被共轭时，最优预编码器的共轭仍然保持最优性，这一数学特性应该被有效利用来提高网络性能。

Method: 提出通用的共轭等变神经网络(CENN)框架。首先建立理论分析，证明统一预编码问题类别的最优策略满足CE性质。针对线性处理函数的DNN无法学习最优预编码策略的问题，开发了一种通用的非线性构造方法，该方法能够将任意基函数转换为满足CE的处理函数，同时保持基函数原有的等变性。这一构造使现有等变网络无需增加可学习参数即可集成CE特性。

Result: 在完全数字预编码和RIS辅助预编码场景的仿真验证显示：CE增强网络相比原始网络在多个方面表现更优，包括更高的学习和泛化性能、更少的训练样本需求以及更短的训练时间。证明了所提出的CENN框架能够有效利用CE这一数学性质来改进预编码学习任务。

Conclusion: 本文成功将共轭等变性这一数学性质引入DNN设计，为解决预编码学习问题提供了新的理论框架和实用方法。通过开发通用非线性构造，在不增加模型复杂度的前提下实现了CE集成的可行性。研究成果为无线网络中的深度学习应用开辟了新的方向，证明了数学性质的利用对于提升深度学习在通信领域应用效果的重要性。

Abstract: Exploiting mathematical properties of wireless policies in deep neural network (DNN) design can improve learning performance and generalizability while reducing training complexity. Permutation equivariance and permutation invariance have been incorporated into DNN architectures. In this paper, we investigate conjugation equivariance (CE) and propose a general conjugation-equivariant neural network (CENN) framework for precoder learning. We first establish that the optimal policies for a unified class of precoding problems satisfy CE, i.e., when the channel matrices are conjugated, the conjugate of an optimal precoder remains optimal. We then show that, for DNNs with linear processing functions, enforcing CE restricts their ability to learn optimal precoding policies. To overcome this limitation, we develop a general nonlinear construction and prove that it converts an arbitrary base function into a CE processing function while preserving the base function's original equivariance properties. This construction enables existing equivariant networks to incorporate CE without adding learnable parameters. Simulations for fully digital and RIS-aided precoding show that the resulting CE-enhanced networks improve learning and generalization performance while requiring fewer training samples and shorter training time than their original counterparts.

</details>


### [107] [Standard-Compliant Neuromorphic Integrated Sensing and Communications Aided by an Intelligent Reflecting Surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13985)
*Jiho Park,Jiechen Chen,Joonhyuk Kang,Osvaldo Simeone*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种符合IEEE 802.15.4z标准的神经形态集成感知与通信(N-ISAC)系统，利用单一脉冲神经网络(SNN)接收机同时解调数字数据并检测无源雷达目标。研究考虑了完整的标准化物理层和基于可重构智能表面(RIS)的真实射线追踪信道模型，分析了RIS频率选择性对超宽带(ISAC)性能的影响，并量化了ISAC性能与接收机计算能量之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经形态计算能够实现事件驱动、低功耗推理，非常适合资源受限的无线设备执行通信、感知和处理任务。脉冲无线电超宽带(IR-UWB)信号采用IEEE 802.15.4标准，与神经形态处理天然匹配，因为两者都基于稀疏时间事件或脉冲操作。本文旨在研究一种标准兼容的神经形态集成感知与通信系统。

Method: 本文研究使用单一脉冲神经网络(SNN)接收机从公共IEEE 802.15.4z高速脉冲重复频率(HRP)超宽带波形中同时解调数字数据和检测无源雷达目标的N-ISAC系统。不同于以往假设简单脉冲位置调制接口和简化多径信道的N-ISAC工作，本文考虑完整的标准化物理层以及由可重构智能表面(RIS)辅助的真实射线追踪信道。模型考虑了基于超材料RIS的频率选择性响应，该响应会分散宽带IR-UWB脉冲，可能同时降低通信和感知性能。通过数值实验量化RIS频率选择性对UWB ISAC的影响，并表征ISAC性能与接收机计算能量之间的权衡关系。

Result: 数值实验量化了RIS频率选择性对UWB ISAC性能的影响，包括通信和数据检测以及无源雷达目标检测的性能变化。研究揭示了ISAC性能与接收机计算能量之间的权衡关系，表明在考虑RIS频率选择性效应时需要在感知/通信性能与计算能效之间取得平衡。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一个完整的标准兼容神经形态集成感知与通信系统框架，首次考虑了真实信道条件和RIS频率选择性效应。研究表明，在超宽带神经形态ISAC系统中，RIS的频率选择性特性会同时影响通信和感知性能，需要在系统设计中仔细平衡性能与能量消耗。该工作为未来基于神经形态计算的集成感知与通信系统提供了重要理论基础。

Abstract: Neuromorphic computing enables event-driven, low-power inference and is therefore an attractive technology for jointly carrying out communication, sensing, and processing at resource-constrained wireless devices. Impulse radio ultra-wideband (IR-UWB) signaling, which is standardized in the IEEE 802.15.4 family and widely deployed for ranging and short-range communication, is naturally matched to neuromorphic processing, since both operate through sparse temporal events, or spikes. In this context, this paper studies a standard-compliant neuromorphic integrated sensing and communications (N-ISAC) system in which a single spiking neural network (SNN) receiver jointly demodulates digital data and detects a passive radar target directly from a common IEEE 802.15.4z high-rate-pulse-repetition-frequency (HRP) UWB waveform. Unlike prior N-ISAC work, which assumed a basic pulse-position-modulation interface and a simplified multipath channel, we consider the full standardized physical layer together with a realistic ray-traced channel aided by a reconfigurable intelligent surface (RIS). The model accounts for the frequency-selective response of a metamaterial-based RIS, which disperses the wideband IR-UWB pulses and can degrade both communication and sensing. Numerical experiments quantify the impact of RIS frequency selectivity on UWB ISAC and characterize the trade-off between ISAC performance and receiver computation energy.

</details>


### [108] [Integrated Sensing, Communication, and Computing in Multi-Tier Systems: Joint Hybrid Beamforming Design and Computation Resource Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14086)
*Peng Liu,Zesong Fei,Xinyi Wang,Qiao Qi,Zhaohui Yang,Meng Hua,Arumugam Nallanathan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种集成感知、通信和计算的协同框架，通过信号复用实现被动感知，并利用分割推理和混合波束成形技术解决资源冲突，最终通过联合优化算法显著降低了计算延迟并保证了感知性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在云-边缘-设备协同架构中，利用上行信号实现被动感知虽无额外开销，但存在通信效率与感知覆盖的权衡。同时，感知任务的引入加剧了MEC服务器的计算资源竞争，导致延迟敏感任务与感知推理任务性能受损。

Method: 提出了一种基于云-边缘-设备协同架构的集成感知、通信和计算（ISCC）框架。采用混合波束成形架构处理硬件约束，引入分割推理机制解决MEC服务器资源竞争。建立联合优化问题以最小化计算延迟，并设计了一种交替优化算法，包括确定最优DNN分割点的双层框架以及基于WMMSE和流形优化的混合波束成形设计方法。

Result: 数值实验结果表明，与基准方案相比，所提出的框架在感知精度与计算延迟之间实现了更优的权衡，验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的ISCC框架通过联合优化感知、通信和计算资源，有效解决了感知与通信之间的权衡以及MEC服务器的资源竞争问题，显著提升了系统的综合性能。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel integrated sensing, communication, and computing (ISCC) framework over a cloud-edge-device collaborative architecture, where passive sensing is enabled by reusing uplink offloading signals to extract sensing information directly at the edge without incurring additional transmission overhead. Nevertheless, such signal reuse introduces an inherent tradeoff between communication efficiency and sensing coverage. To address this challenge, we adopt a hybrid beamforming architecture under practical hardware constraints. In addition, the integration of sensing tasks creates significant resource contention at the mobile edge computing (MEC) server, where latency-sensitive device tasks and computation-intensive sensing inference tasks compete for limited processing capacity. To alleviate this computation burden, we introduce a split inference mechanism that strategically partitions intelligent sensing tasks between the edge and the cloud. Building upon this framework, we formulate a joint optimization problem to minimize the average computation latency of all device tasks subject to strict sensing performance constraints. To tackle the high non-convexity of the formulated problem, we develop an efficient alternating optimization algorithm. In particular, we design a two-layer framework to jointly determine the optimal DNN splitting point and computation resource allocation and employ a weighted minimum mean square error (WMMSE)-based approach with manifold optimization for hybrid beamforming design. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves a superior tradeoff between sensing accuracy and computation latency compared to the benchmark schemes.

</details>


### [109] [An HFM-Inspired Random Access Preamble Design for NTN under High Doppler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14168)
*Mehwish Bibi,Saira Rafique,Ahmed Naeem,Huseyin Arslan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出基于双曲调频(HFM)的PRACH前导码设计，解决低轨非地面网络(LEO NTN)中大多普勒偏移和延迟不确定性导致的同步与识别问题，通过匹配滤波检测实现性能提升，优于现有ZC和LFM方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低轨非地面网络的高轨道速度和长传播距离引发显著多普勒频移与延迟不确定性，导致传统Zadoff Chu(ZC)和线性调频(LFM)前导码在多普勒模糊与延迟-多普勒耦合下出现定时估计误差增大及前导识别性能下降，需设计鲁棒性更强的前导方案。

Method: 设计HFM激励的PRACH前导码，采用匹配滤波器接收方案以体现前导设计增益；建立统一的延迟-多普勒模糊函数框架，分析ZC/LFM/HFM前导的自相关与互相关特性；提出基于缩放因子的码本设计以确保多用户可分性。

Result: NTN信道仿真表明，相比ZC和频域叠加LFM基线方案，HFM前导实现更高检测概率、更低定时均方根误差(RMSE)，以及改善的峰旁瓣比和积分旁瓣电平性能。

Conclusion: HFM激励PRACH前导在无补偿或未知多普勒环境下显著提升同步鲁棒性和识别可靠性，通过模糊函数分析与码本优化验证其优越性，满足6G非地面网络物理层接入需求。

Abstract: Non-terrestrial networks (NTNs) are a key enabler of ubiquitous 6G connectivity, but the high orbital velocity and long propagation distances in low-Earth orbit (LEO) NTN operation introduce large Doppler shifts and substantial delay uncertainty that challenge New Radio (NR) physical random access channel (PRACH) design. Conventional Zadoff Chu (ZC) and linear frequency modulated (LFM) preambles are particularly vulnerable, as Doppler induced ambiguity and delay Doppler coupling degrade timing estimation and preamble identification. This paper proposes a hyperbolic frequency modulation (HFM) inspired PRACH preamble for robust synchronization and reliable identification under uncompensated or unknown Doppler, detected with a conventional matched filter receiver so that the gains reflect the preamble design. A unified delay Doppler ambiguity function framework characterizes the self and cross ambiguity behavior of ZC, LFM, and HFM inspired preambles, and a scaling-factor based codebook ensures multi-user separability. Simulation results under NTN channel conditions confirm higher detection probability, lower timing root mean square error (RMSE), and improved peak to sidelobe and integrated sidelobe levels compared with ZC and frequency-domain superposed LFM baselines.

</details>


### [110] [Budget-Aware Federated Dual-Side Channel Estimation for Hybrid mmWave Massive MIMO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14182)
*Jiawei Chen,Ruining Fan,Mouli Chakraborty,Avishek Nag,Anshu Mukherjee*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出BARRNet（预算感知重校准细化网络），用于混合mmWave大规模MIMO系统中通信受限的联邦学习CSI估计，在-13 dB下行NMSE目标下相比基线方法降低28.9%的通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合波束成形导致压缩且有噪声的观测，使精确CSI恢复具有挑战性；联邦学习中的重复模型交换使得通信效率强烈依赖于估计器大小，需要在有限参数预算下有效设计估计器。

Method: 基于标准FedAvg协议（而非开发新的联邦优化算法），提出BARRNet，将紧凑残差骨干网络与轻量级通道重校准模块结合，实现双侧CSI细化，并针对重复模型交换场景优化有限模型容量的使用。

Result: 仿真结果表明BARRNet在NMSE-通信权衡上优于仅骨干网络控制和更重的CNN基线；在5 dB SNR下，达到-13 dB下行NMSE目标时，相比架构匹配的仅骨干控制方法累计通信开销减少28.9%。

Conclusion: 通信高效的联邦CSI估计不仅依赖于模型紧凑性，更取决于在重复模型交换条件下如何有效利用有限的模型容量。

Abstract: This work studies communication-constrained federated dual-side channel state information (CSI) estimation in hybrid millimeter-wave (mmWave) massive multiple input multiple output (MIMO) systems. Accurate CSI recovery is challenging because hybrid beamforming yields compressed and noisy observations, while repeated model exchange in federated learning (FL) makes communication efficiency strongly dependent on estimator size. Rather than developing a new federated optimization algorithm, we focus on estimator design under standard federated averaging (FedAvg) and study how to use a limited parameter budget effectively under repeated model exchange. Based on this perspective, we propose a budget-aware recalibrated refinement network (BARRNet), which combines a compact residual backbone with lightweight channel-wise recalibration for dual-side CSI refinement. Simulation results show that BARRNet achieves a better normalized mean squared error (NMSE)--communication tradeoff than the backbone-only control and heavier convolutional neural network (CNN) baselines. At 5 dB SNR, for the -13 dB DL NMSE target, it reduces the cumulative communication required by 28.9\% relative to the architecture-matched backbone-only control. These results indicate that communication-efficient federated CSI estimation depends not only on model compactness, but also on how limited model capacity is used under repeated model exchange.

</details>


### [111] [Why Performance Metrics Overpromise in Auditory Attention Decoding: an Information-Theoretic Reappraisal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14250)
*Nicolas Heintz,Simon Geirnaert,Tom Francart,Alexander Bertrand*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 论文提出相对增量互信息(rIMI)指标，用于评估听觉注意力解码(AAD)算法的真实性能，发现直接分类型AAD算法并不比传统刺激重构算法优越，且其准确率被无关特征漂移人为夸大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AAD算法评估主要在稳态条件下使用准确率和信息传输率等指标，但这些指标无法考虑预测之间的依赖性，可能导致对AAD算法真实性能的严重错误表征。

Method: 引入相对增量互信息(rIMI)指标，衡量新预测消除对关注说话者身份剩余不确定性的速率，能够追踪预测实际生成的新有用信息量；分析rIMI和注意力切换时AAD模型的行为；对比直接分类型和传统刺激重构型AAD算法。

Result: 1) 直接分类型AAD算法并不优于传统的刺激重构型AAD算法，尽管单独看准确率可能暗示相反结论；2) 直接分类型AAD预测受到无关特征漂移的严重影响，通过在不同时间窗口甚至不同试验间泄露信息，人为地夸大了准确率。

Conclusion: 在AAD算法评估中必须考虑预测间的依赖性，否则会严重错误表征算法的真实性能；rIMI作为新指标能够有效揭示这一依赖性问题，并更准确地评估AAD算法的实际性能。

Abstract: Auditory attention decoding (AAD) algorithms are predominantly evaluated in a steady state where a listener continuously attends to the same speaker, using metrics such as accuracy and information transfer rate. However, such metrics fail to account for the (in-)dependence of an AAD prediction with respect to previous predictions. In this paper, we argue that failing to take this dependence into account in the algorithm evaluation can lead to severe misrepresentations of the true performance of an AAD algorithm.
  We therefore introduce the relative Incremental Mutual Information (rIMI); the rate at which a new prediction removes the remaining uncertainty about the identity of the attended speaker. This allows us to track how much new, useful information a prediction actually generates on top of the information already obtained from previous predictions. By investigating the rIMI and the behaviour of AAD models around attention switches, we demonstrate that recent direct-classification AAD algorithms are not superior to traditional AAD algorithms based on stimulus reconstruction, despite what accuracy alone may suggest. We also demonstrate how these direct-classification AAD predictions are severely influenced by irrelevant feature drifts, which artificially inflates accuracies by leaking information across windows, and even across trials.

</details>


### [112] [LLM-Assisted LDPC Decoding via Syndrome-Verified Semantic Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14280)
*Sojeong Park,Hyeonsu Lyu,Jaehyun Choi,Hyun Jong Yang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于大语言模型(LLM)辅助的低密度奇偶校验(LDPC)码解码框架，通过验证LLM预测而非直接信任其修正结果，将语义知识可靠地转化为物理层可靠性增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义通信利用有效载荷的含义信息，而比特级处理会丢弃这些信息。当自然语言有效载荷的频道解码失败时，错误表现为恢复文本中的损坏字符。虽然LLM可以从语义上下文中推断出预期字符，但也可能产生不正确的修正，直接应用会引入新的比特错误。

Method: 提出一种LLM辅助的LDPC码解码框架：(1)不直接信任LLM预测，而是将修改后的字符与奇偶校验约束一起评估；(2)仅接受已验证的修正作为可靠的语义先验；(3)将这些先验作为软更新注入到频道对数似然比中，保留原始频道证据且不修改解码器；(4)通过后续的置信传播将注入的证据分配到校验节点，不仅恢复注入的比特，还恢复LLM未能修正的残余错误。

Result: 仿真结果表明，在2.0 dB信噪比下，该方法相比传统解码器实现了73%的比特错误率降低；而将传统解码器迭代次数加倍至相同预算仅能获得21%的改善。即使LLM预测不准确，验证过程仍能保持高于0.88的注入精度。

Conclusion: 语义知识可以被可靠地转化为物理层可靠性增益。该框架能够利用LLM的强大能力，同时避免因不正确的修正而引入新的比特错误，为语义通信提供了有效的实现方案。

Abstract: Semantic communication exploits the meaning of the payload, which bit-level processing discards. When channel decoding fails on a natural language payload, the errors appear as corrupted characters in the recovered text. A large language model (LLM) infers the intended characters from the semantic context, but it can also produce incorrect corrections. Applying them directly introduces new bit errors when the LLM modifies characters incorrectly. In this paper, we propose an LLM-assisted decoding framework for low-density parity-check (LDPC) codes. Rather than trusting LLM predictions, the decoder evaluates the modified characters jointly against the parity-check constraints and admits only the accepted corrections as verified semantic priors. These priors are injected as soft updates to the channel log-likelihood ratios, preserving the original channel evidence without modifying the decoder. A subsequent belief propagation pass distributes the injected evidence across the check nodes, recovering not only the injected bits but also the residual errors that the LLM fails to correct. Simulations demonstrate a 73% bit error rate reduction over a conventional decoder at 2.0 dB, whereas doubling its iterations to the same budget yields only 21%. The verification maintains an injection precision above 0.88 despite inaccurate LLM predictions, demonstrating that semantic knowledge can be reliably translated into physical-layer reliability gains.

</details>


### [113] [Lightweight Beam Index Map Using Coupled Gaussian Mixture Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14301)
*Amar Kasibovic,Franz Weißer,Wolfgang Utschick*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: This paper addresses the beam alignment problem in MIMO systems from a decentralized, mobile terminal (MT)-centric perspective. We propose a lightweight machine learning approach that leverages position information to perform beam selection without relying on exhaustive search or strong base station coordination. Specifically, we model the joint distribution of MT positions and channel observations using a coupled Gaussian mixture model (GMM), enabling the construction of a beam index map (BIM) that directly associates spatial locations with codebook entries. To account for practical hardware constraints, we introduce a refinement procedure that adapts the learned statistical model to fixed codebooks. The resulting method is computationally efficient and suitable for deployment on resource-constrained devices. Simulation results on the DeepMIMO and QuaDRiGa datasets demonstrate that the proposed approach outperforms clustering-based fingerprinting methods and achieves competitive performance compared to exhaustive search, while significantly reducing complexity and overhead.

</details>


### [114] [Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Joint Handover Management and Power Allocation in Multi-Orbit Satellite Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14335)
*Yassine Afif,Ashutosh Balakrishnan,Philippe Martins,Mohammed Almekhlafi,Antoine Lesage-Landry,Gunes Karabulut Kurt*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究未来6G非地面网络中LEO、MEO、GEO多轨道卫星的用户关联与资源分配问题，采用多智能体强化学习方法在吞吐量与切换频率之间实现有效平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来6G非地面网络将融合低地球轨道(LEO)、中地球轨道(MEO)和地球静止轨道(GEO)卫星。由于各轨道层具有互补特性，需要通过联合用户关联、功率分配和切换管理进行协调，尤其在LEO快速动态环境下这一优化问题极具挑战性。

Method: 将问题建模为混合整数非线性规划，并分解为两个子问题：1)使用多智能体强化学习(MARL)策略选择用户关联；2)在每个时隙精确求解凸功率分配子问题，其结果作为MARL部分的奖励。MARL策略采用多智能体近端策略优化(MAPPO)和定向多智能体通信(TarMAC)机制训练，状态编码轨道层相关的切换惩罚以使策略感知轨道层信息。

Result: 在基于真实两行要素数据构建的蒙巴萨市多星座场景中评估。所提策略达到贪心信噪比(SNR)最大化方案92%的吞吐量，同时触发次数减少四倍以上；相比保守停留启发式算法吞吐量提升约14%。与相同架构的LEO-only学习策略相比，通过将部分用户卸载到MEO和GEO层，实现了略高的吞吐量和更少的切换，呈现出有利的多轨道行为。

Conclusion: 基于MARL的方法能有效解决多轨道协调问题，通过涌现的多轨道用户关联行为实现吞吐量与切换频率的良好权衡。

Abstract: Future sixth-generation non-terrestrial networks are expected to combine low Earth orbit (LEO), medium Earth orbit (MEO), and geostationary Earth orbit (GEO) satellites, whose complementary layers must be coordinated through joint user association, power allocation, and handover management under fast LEO dynamics. This paper studies this problem by formulating it as a mixed-integer nonlinear program and decomposing it into a multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) policy that selects the associations and a convex power-allocation subproblem solved exactly at each time slot that defines the reward of the MARL part. The association policy is trained with multi-agent proximal policy optimization (MAPPO) and the targeted multi-agent communication (TarMAC) mechanism, and is made aware of the orbital layer through a state that encodes layer-dependent handover penalties. Evaluated on a realistic multi-constellation scenario built from real two-line element data over Nairobi, Kenya, the proposed policy reaches 92% of the throughput of a greedy signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) maximizing scheme while triggering more than four times fewer handovers, and improves throughput by roughly 14% over a conservative stay heuristic. Compared to an LEO-only learned policy of identical architecture, it attains slightly higher throughput with fewer handovers by offloading a fraction of the users to the MEO and GEO layers, an emergent multi-orbit behavior that drives its favorable throughput and handover trade-off.

</details>


### [115] [Sensing-Aided Ordered Reliability Bits Guessing Random Additive Noise Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14479)
*Yu Ge,Lukas Rapp,Ken R. Duffy,Muriel Médard*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了集成感知与通信(ISAC)如何改进信道解码，通过将环境感知信息与导频观测融合，构建基于几何的信道系数先验，利用LMMSE估计得到后验信道估计及不确定性，进而计算对数似然比(LLRs)供给ORBGRAND解码器，提升了噪声猜测过程的可靠性排序，在导频受限场景下效果尤为显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集成感知与通信(ISAC)能够为未来无线系统提供环境信息，支持超越传统数据传输的任务。然而，ISAC对信道解码的影响尚未得到充分探索。因此，本文旨在研究ISAC如何提升信道解码性能，特别是在资源受限场景下的优势。

Method: 本文研究了单输入单输出(SISO)窄带衰落信道上基于感知辅助的有序可靠性比特猜测随机加性噪声解码(ORBGRAND)。具体方法为：利用环境信息构建信道系数的基于几何的先验分布，通过线性最小均方误差(LMMSE)估计将该先验与导频观测相融合，得到后验信道估计及其不确定性；利用该后验估计和不确定性计算供给ORBGRAND的对数似然比(LLRs)，从而改进驱动噪声猜测过程的可靠性排序。

Result: 仿真结果表明，感知辅助的ORBGRAND能够降低分组错误率并减少平均查询复杂度。在导频受限场景下，性能增益最为显著。

Conclusion: 本文验证了ISAC感知信息能够有效提升信道解码性能，特别是在导频受限场景下，通过融合环境信息与导频观测构建更精确的信道估计，显著改善了ORBGRAND解码的可靠性排序与整体解码效率。

Abstract: Integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) is a key enabler for future wireless systems, providing environmental information that can support tasks beyond conventional data transmission. However, its impact on channel decoding remains less explored. This paper studies sensing-aided ordered reliability bits guessing random additive noise decoding (ORBGRAND) over single-input single-output narrowband fading channels. Environmental information is used to construct a geometry-based prior for the channel coefficient, which is fused with pilot observations via linear minimum mean square error (LMMSE) estimation. The resulting posterior channel estimate and uncertainty are used to compute the log-likelihood ratios (LLRs) supplied to ORBGRAND, improving the reliability ordering that drives its noise-guessing process. Simulation results demonstrate improved block error rate and reduced average query complexity, with the largest gains in pilot-limited regimes.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [Adjacency-Based Spectral Proxy Control of Mobile Communication Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13616)
*Mariana del Castillo,Federico Larroca*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出A-Fiedler方法，用邻接矩阵的主特征向量替代Fiedler向量，实现了更简单的分布式网络控制方案，在保持网络性能的同时提高了分布式估计的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究异质移动智能体网络中通信智能体的在线重定位问题。传统基于谱图指标（如代数连通性）的控制方法依赖Fiedler向量，其分布式估计需要无界通信轮次才能收敛，不适合实时控制应用。

Method: 通过结构分解将Fiedler梯度控制器分解为局部交互规则和图嵌入组件，提出A-Fiedler方法，用邻接矩阵的主特征向量替代Fiedler嵌入，该表示法更易于在局部通信约束下进行分布式实现。

Result: 实验表明A-Fiedler在无通信约束情况下与经典Fiedler梯度控制器性能相当，在分布式估计约束下表现出更好的鲁棒性。相同通信轮次下，Fiedler梯度可能收敛到不连通配置，而A-Fiedler能保持性能。

Conclusion: 该工作为分布式网络控制提供了更简单的实现路径，通过替代性的图嵌入方法降低了分布式估计的复杂性，使实时移动智能体网络控制更加实用可行。

Abstract: We consider a heterogeneous mobile-agent network composed of uncontrolled task agents and controllable communication agents. The objective is to reposition communication agents online as task agents move. Since throughput-based objectives are generally unsuitable for real-time control, spectral graph metrics such as algebraic connectivity are commonly adopted as surrogate objectives. However, controlling algebraic connectivity relies on the eigenvector corresponding to the second-smallest eigenvalue of a graph's Laplacian matrix (i.e., the Fiedler vector), whose distributed estimation requires an unbounded number of communication rounds to converge.
  In this work, we identify a structural decomposition of this Fiedler-gradient controller into a local interaction rule and a graph embedding component, suggesting the use of alternative embeddings that are easier to estimate distributively than the Fiedler vector. As a particular instance, we propose A-Fiedler, which replaces the Fiedler embedding with the dominant eigenvector of the adjacency matrix, commonly used as a graph embedding of nodes into a latent geometry. This representation is more naturally suited for distributed implementation under local communication constraints.
  We evaluate A-Fiedler against the classical Fiedler-gradient controller. Results show comparable network performance in the absence of communication constraints and improved robustness under distributed estimation. For instance, under the same number of communication rounds, the Fielder-gradient may even converge to disconnected configurations whereas our proposition maintains performance. We believe our contribution provides a simpler path toward distributed network control.

</details>


### [117] [hint$^2$: Hierarchical World Models for Inference-Time Temporal Logic Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13678)
*Moritz Zoellner,Anastasios Manganaris,Ahmed H. Qureshi,Rohan Paleja*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出hint²方法，利用分层世界模型在推理时引导短视界策略满足复杂的线性时间逻辑(LTL)规范。通过高层模型预测原子命题转移以指导LTL自动机进展，低层模型预测即时状态演化以保证局部安全性。实验表明该方法在CALVIN上优于现有推理时引导方法，并能真实操作UR5e机械臂完成复杂指令。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习的核心目标是使机器人能够执行运行时指定的丰富指令。尽管大规模语言条件策略已取得进展，但在时间结构和安全性约束方面仍存在困难。线性时间逻辑(LTL)虽能表达复杂的非马尔可夫指令，但如何将现代短视界策略引导至满足LTL规范仍具挑战性，因为这些策略生成短视界动作块并在闭环中重新规划，而LTL规范通常评估长视界轨迹。

Method: 作者提出hint²方法，利用分层世界模型在推理时引导短视界策略满足复杂LTL规范。关键思想是基于每个世界模型的抽象层次推导两个独立的引导目标：高层模型预测动作引起的与任务相关的原子命题未来转移，以指导LTL自动机进展；低层动力学模型预测即时状态演化，用于准确的局部安全性引导。

Result: 实验表明hint²克服了当前LTL引导扩散方法的局限性，在CALVIN基准上优于现有推理时引导方法，并能更优雅地完成具有复杂活性(safety)和安全性约束的指令，胜过语言条件替代方案。此外，在真实UR5e机械臂上成功处理复杂指令。

Conclusion: hint²方法通过分层世界模型在推理时有效引导短视界策略满足复杂LTL规范，解决了短视界策略与长视界LTL评估之间的鸿沟，在仿真和真实机械臂实验中均证明了其优越性和实用性。

Abstract: A central goal of robot learning is to enable robots to execute rich instructions specified at runtime. Large-scale language-conditioned policies have made substantial progress toward this goal, yet still struggle with temporal structure and safety constraints. Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) provides a powerful language to express complex, non-Markovian instructions. However, guiding learned manipulation policies toward LTL satisfaction remains challenging because modern policies generate short-horizon action chunks and replan in closed loop, while almost all LTL specifications are evaluated over long-horizon trajectories. In this paper, we introduce hint$^2$, a method for guiding short-horizon policies toward satisfying complex LTL specifications at inference time using hierarchical world models. Our key idea is to derive two separate guidance objectives using each world model's abstraction level. A high-level model predicts future action-induced transitions in task-relevant atomic propositions to guide progress through the LTL automaton, while a low-level dynamics model predicts immediate state evolution for accurate local safety guidance. Our results show that hint$^2$ overcomes the limitations of current LTL-guided diffusion methods, outperforms existing inference-time steering methods in CALVIN, and successfully completes instructions with complex liveness and safety constraints more elegantly than language-conditioned alternatives. Finally, we demonstrate that hint$^2$ can handle complex instructions on a real UR5e manipulator.

</details>


### [118] [Graph-MambaNav: Spatial-Temporal Graph Mamba Leveraging Object-Relation Knowledge for Object-Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13723)
*Leyuan Sun,Genxin Chen,Linwei Ye,Yan Zhang,Xi Kan,Yanfei Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Graph-MambaNav，一种基于目标感知的时空图编码框架，通过基于目标相关性的节点排序和基于LLM的常识关系初始化，在未见环境中提升物体导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图方法在特征或注意力层面引入目标感知，但缺乏控制信息传播顺序的显式机制，限制了目标相关重要性建模和长程依赖能力。Graph-Mamba强调通过序列排序进行节点优先级划分对全局推理至关重要，因此本文研究节点优先级机制及其在物体导航中的作用。

Method: Graph-MambaNav框架引入基于目标相关性的启发式对象排序，使更具信息的对象在后期处理以聚合更丰富上下文。节点排序和边权重初始化自LLM推导的常识对象关系。空间模块结合局部消息传递和全局Graph-Mamba选择扫描；时间模块对对象时间顺序应用Mamba序列建模，实现长程时间推理的选择性历史上下文聚合。

Result: 在AI2-THOR和RoboTHOR上的实验表明导航性能提升且具有泛化性，真实世界机器人部署进一步验证了方法有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的Graph-MambaNav通过目标感知的节点优先级机制和时空图编码，显著改善了物体导航任务的性能和泛化能力，为未见环境中的高效决策提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Object-goal navigation requires an agent to reason over object relationships and prioritize target-relevant objects for efficient decision making in unseen environments. While existing graph-based methods incorporate target-awareness at the feature or attention level, they remain permutation-invariant and lack an explicit mechanism to control information propagation order, limiting their ability to model target-dependent importance and long-range dependencies. In contrast, Graph-Mamba highlights that node prioritization through sequence ordering is critical for effective global reasoning. In this work, we investigate the node prioritization mechanism in Graph-Mamba and study its role in object navigation. We propose Graph-MambaNav, a target-aware spatial-temporal graph encoding framework that introduces a heuristic ordering over objects based on their relevance to the target, allowing more informative objects to be processed later to aggregate richer context. Both node ordering and edge weights are initialized from LLM-derived commonsense object relationships, providing a unified prior for structured reasoning. A spatial module integrates local message passing with global GraphMamba-based selective scanning, while a temporal module applies Mamba-based sequence modeling over object-wise temporal orders, allowing selective aggregation of historical context for long-range temporal reasoning. Experiments on AI2-THOR and RoboTHOR demonstrate improved navigation performance with generalization, and additional real-world robot deployment further validates the effectiveness of our proposed approach.

</details>


### [119] [Knowledge-Data-Dual-Driven Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous Vehicle Control in Mixed Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13878)
*Jie Fang,Wei Zheng,Mengyun Xu,Eui-Jin Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出知识-数据双驱动强化学习(KDDRL)框架，解决自动驾驶汽车在混合交通中决策的三个挑战：强化学习模型难以捕捉交互车辆意图；周围车辆突发动作导致非平稳性和长尾安全事件；混合动作空间因不同时间尺度而不稳定。该方法通过条件生成模型合成意图感知的轨迹，结合知识驱动与数据驱动范式，并设计耦合模块实现异步多时间尺度优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶汽车在混合交通环境中面临三个相互关联的决策挑战：(1)物理先验融入强化学习模型时，无法有效捕捉潜在的交互车辆意图和多样化驾驶员行为，限制了主动推理能力；(2)周围车辆的突发动作导致环境非平稳性，使长尾安全事件研究不足；(3)连续跟车和离散变道的不同时间尺度使混合动作空间不稳定，影响统一强化学习训练。

Method: 本文提出知识-数据双驱动强化学习(KDDRL)方法，包含三个核心组件：(1)条件深度生成模型合成意图感知未来轨迹，将被动感知转化为主动预测状态；(2)知识-数据双驱动范式运行于这些预测状态，融合概率数据驱动洞察与物理约束，引导安全关键场景中的安全探索；(3)耦合模块将意图感知轨迹和物理约束压缩为紧凑共享嵌入，实现连续跟车和离散变道的异步多时间尺度优化，同时保持互信息。

Result: 在数据集校准的仿真环境中评估表明，KDDRL能有效处理意图不确定性，加速训练收敛，并在安全性、效率和舒适性方面优于传统基线方法。该方法解决了混合交通中自动驾驶决策的关键挑战。

Conclusion: KDDRL框架通过结合生成模型、双驱动范式和耦合模块，成功应对了混合交通中自动驾驶决策的三大挑战。该方法提升了处理意图不确定性的能力，加速了训练过程，并在多项性能指标上超过现有基线，为自动驾驶在复杂交通环境中的安全高效运行提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: In mixed traffic, decision-making for autonomous vehicles (AVs) confronts three interrelated challenges. First, physics-based priors incorporated into reinforcement learning (RL) models fail to capture latent interactive vehicle intentions and diverse driver behaviors, limiting the proactive reasoning capabilities. Second, abrupt maneuvers by surrounding vehicles cause non-stationarity, leaving long-tail safety events under-explored. Third, hybrid action spaces destabilize unified RL training due to the different temporal scales of continuous car-following and discrete lane-changing maneuvers. To address these issues, we propose Knowledge-Data Dual-driven Reinforcement Learning (KDDRL). First, a conditional deep generative model synthesizes intention-aware future trajectories, converting passive perception into proactive predictive states. Second, a knowledge-data dual-driven paradigm operates on these predictive states, fusing probabilistic data-driven insights with physical constraints to guide safe exploration through safety-critical scenarios. Third, a coupling module compresses both intention-aware trajectories and physical constraints into compact shared embeddings. This unified representation enables asynchronous multi-timescale optimization of continuous car-following and discrete lane-changing while preserving mutual information. Evaluations on dataset-calibrated simulations demonstrate that KDDRL effectively handles intention uncertainty, accelerates training convergence, and outperforms conventional baseline methods in terms of safety, efficiency, and comfort.

</details>


### [120] [Ontology-Grounded World Models for Failure Diagnosis and Closed-Loop Repair in Physical AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13901)
*Kailin Wang,Haoxiang Jie,Yaoyuan Yan,Jiacheng Zhou,Zhiyou Heng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Onto-EV-WM，一种基于本体的诊断与验证门控纠错接口，叠加于EV-WM之上，通过任务局部TBox、源特定接地映射和确定性规则记录缺失谓词、关联修正路由并确定接受标准。实验表明，在PointMaze上取得94%成功率且最终状态距离更优，在LIBERO-Goal上实现93.8%-94.05%的修正窗口成功率，在10,030任务的LIBERO-Plus中成功8,526个任务（85.00%），各套件成功率在65.98%-91.39%之间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的EV-WM虽用特征和事件分数表示候选质量，但未能显式记录未满足的任务谓词、可用于纠错机制的路由标签以及修正后的接受结果。这限制了诊断和纠错能力，故需一种基于本体的接口来补充这些语义信息。

Method: 作者实现了一个叠加于EV-WM之上的本体层：(1)定义任务局部TBox，包括实体类型、谓词签名和约束；(2)源特定接地映射将预测或模拟器观测的状态映射到任务ABox；(3)确定性规则在分配路由标签时保留每个缺失谓词及其参数。学习或启发式提议器保持独立于该符号接口，接受由原生任务谓词决定，有限协议决定是否重试失败的验证。

Result: 在PointMaze评估中，EV-WM与Onto-EV-WM均报告94%成功率，但最终状态距离分别为0.90573和0.61177；单独预算搜索达到100%成功率。在LIBERO-Goal上，本体将失败任务条件表示为类型化记录，保留谓词参数，并关联声明的源/联合纠错路由与谓词门控接受，在种子0上实现93.8%修正窗口成功率，跨四个评估采样种子达到94.05±0.30%。在固定的10,030任务LIBERO-Plus注册表中，Onto-EV-WM在8,526个任务上成功（85.00%），套件级成功率分别为LIBERO-10的65.98%、LIBERO-Goal的91.39%、LIBERO-Object的91.38%和LIBERO-Spatial的91.38%。

Conclusion: Onto-EV-WM通过基于本体的符号接口增强了EV-WM的诊断与纠错能力，在多个任务套件上显示出显著的性能提升，尤其在保持高成功率的同时缩短了最终状态距离。然而，本体单独的因果贡献未被单独测量，真实机器人恢复也未评估，这是未来的研究方向。

Abstract: EV-WM represents candidate quality with feature and event scores, but these scores do not explicitly record an unmet task predicate, a route label for an available correction mechanism, or a post-correction acceptance result. We present Onto-EV-WM, an ontology-grounded diagnosis and verification-gated correction interface layered above EV-WM rather than a replacement world-model architecture. The implemented task-local TBox defines entity types, predicate signatures, and constraints; source-specific grounding maps predicted or simulator-observed states to task ABoxes; and deterministic rules retain each missing predicate and its arguments when assigning a route label. Learned or heuristic proposers remain separate from this symbolic interface; native task predicates determine acceptance, and the bounded protocol determines whether a failed verification is retried. In the aligned PointMaze evaluation, EV-WM and Onto-EV-WM both report 94% success, with mean final-state distances of 0.90573 and 0.61177, respectively; the separately budgeted search reaches 100% success. On LIBERO-Goal, the ontology represents failed task conditions as typed records, retains their predicate arguments, and associates them with the declared source/joint correction route and predicate-gated acceptance; the complete configuration reports 93.8% corrected-window success on seed 0 and 94.05 +- 0.30% across four evaluation-sampling seeds. On the fixed 10,030-task LIBERO-Plus registry, Onto-EV-WM succeeds on 8,526 tasks (85.00%), with suite-level success rates of 65.98% for LIBERO-10, 91.39% for LIBERO-Goal, and 91.38% for both LIBERO-Object and LIBERO-Spatial. These numbers report the performance of the complete ontology-grounded configurations under the tested simulator protocols; an ontology-only causal share is not measured separately, and real-robot recovery is not evaluated.

</details>


### [121] [Communication in modular robotic motor control: Bilateral controllers under realistic constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13904)
*Jingwen Li,Levin Kuhlmann,Jason Friedman,Gideon Kowadlo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究具有信号依赖噪声和能耗约束的肌肉骨骼系统中模块化控制器的性能。受大脑双侧半球组织启发，提出了一种基于GRU的双模块循环控制器，通过可学习的延迟半球间通道连接，并在可微双臂肌肉骨骼模拟器中端到端训练。结果表明模块化架构显著优于同等容量的单体基线，且半球间通信能改善末端精度、降低能耗、减少肌肉共收缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人运动控制在肌肉骨骼系统中需要在信号依赖噪声（运动命令方差随命令幅度缩放）和能耗约束下实现快速精准的运动和稳健的姿势稳定。模块化控制器可将这些相互竞争的需求分配给交互的子模块，但在现实约束下其是否优于单体架构，以及模块间通信如何影响策略仍不清楚。

Method: 受大脑双侧半球组织启发，设计了一种基于两个GRU模块的循环控制器，通过可学习的、带延迟的半球间通道连接。在可微双臂肌肉骨骼模拟器中端到端训练，并在达到任务和保持任务中进行评估。

Result: 模块化架构显著优于同等容量的单体基线。与无通信的模块化控制器相比，学习的半球间通信重塑了解决方案：在非零延迟情况下提高了末端精度、降低了能耗、减少了肌肉共收缩。

Conclusion: 对于机器人技术，受生物启发的模块化控制器为实现噪声和能耗约束下的稳健运动提供了实用途径，模块间通信提供了调整精度、稳定性和驱动成本之间权衡的机制。

Abstract: Robotic motor control in musculoskeletal systems requires fast, accurate movement and robust postural stabilization under signal-dependent noise (where motor command variance scales with command magnitude) and energetic cost. Modular controllers can distribute these competing demands across interacting submodules, but it remains unclear whether they outperform monolithic architectures under realistic constraints, and how inter-module communication shapes the resulting strategy. Inspired by the bilateral hemispheric organization of the brain, we introduce a recurrent controller of two GRU-based modules connected by a learnable, delayed inter-hemispheric channel, trained end-to-end in a differentiable two-arm musculoskeletal simulator. Across reaching and holding tasks, the modular architecture substantially outperforms a capacity-matched monolithic baseline. Compared to a matched modular controller without communication, learned inter-hemispheric communication reshapes the solution: improved endpoint precision, lower energetic cost in non-zero-delay regimes, and reduced muscle co-contraction. Our findings show that for robotics, biologically inspired modular controllers offer a practical route to robust movement under noise and energetic constraints, with inter-module communication providing a mechanism to tune trade-offs between precision, stability, and actuation cost.

</details>


### [122] [BICPO-VLA: Behavior-Identified Continuation Preference Optimization for Smooth Asynchronous Vision-Language-Action Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13924)
*Ming Shang,Yuchen Huang,Jiaoyang Chen,Haoyuan Hu,Han Yu,Liping Song,Luyun Feng,Shuo Bao,Wei Dong,Xinzhou Wang,Fuchun Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出BICPO-VLA，通过三步顺序处理解决机器人动作交接中的请求-交接间隙问题，提升动作生成的连续性与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 请求-交接间隙由行为意图歧义、物理状态漂移和交接时残留不匹配导致，影响机器人动作生成的效率与准确性，需针对性方法解决。

Method: 采用指令感知因果历史编码器识别行为意图；利用序列Haar子空间生成分解动作片段为互补系数，实现分阶段生成与精确重建；最后通过已知动作滚动与基于Flow-DPO的参考相关对齐适应残留不匹配。

Result: 实验表明该方法缩短了交接前机器人移动时间，减少迭代细化需求，并提高了动作交接的精度与连贯性。

Conclusion: BICPO-VLA通过三步方法有效缓解请求-交接间隙，提升机器人动作交接的连续性与任务执行可靠性。

Abstract: The request-to-handoff gap has three coupled sources: ambiguity about the behavior intended at request time, physical-state drift accumulated during action generation, and residual incompatibility when the new action finally assumes control. BICPO-VLA addresses them in sequence. First, an instruction-aware causal history encoder identifies the behavior supported by the command and current task progress. Second, sequential Haar subspace generation decomposes each action chunk into complementary pairwise scaffold and residual coefficients, enabling two specialized generation stages followed by exact reconstruction. By reducing iterative refinement in the original action space, it shortens the interval over which the robot continues moving before the new chunk becomes available. Finally, BICPO rolls the known outgoing actions to the actual handoff state and applies reference-relative Flow-DPO among behaviorally matched candidates, adapting the generated chunk to the remaining request-to-handoff mismatch without changing its intended behavior.

</details>


### [123] [AdvDex: Learning Dexterous Manipulation from Human Demonstrations via Joint-Aligned Actions and Adversarial Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14028)
*Zhiyue Zhao,Jingyi Wu,Hairuo Liu,Mingyu Liu,Liyang Li,Hengdi Zhang,Tong He,Zhengxue Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AdvDex是一种统一的视觉-语言-动作(VLA)框架，通过OmniShare大规模人类操作数据集、关节对齐动作空间(JAAS)和域对抗学习，实现从人类和机器人演示中学习效果良好的灵巧操作，并支持跨实体泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作是具身智能的基本能力，但扩展面临三大挑战：1）机器人演示采集成本高；2）不同实体的动作空间存在差异；3）基于异构数据训练的策略容易将任务相关的视觉线索与实体特定的外观特征纠缠，限制了跨实体泛化能力。

Method: AdvDex框架包含三个核心组件：1）OmniShare——大规模多模态人类操作演示数据集，提供高质量运动学监督和触觉测量，减少对机器人遥操作的依赖；2）关节对齐动作空间(JAAS)——包含SE(3)腕部位姿和15个手指关节的规范动作表示，实现人类手、灵巧机器人手和并联夹持器的功能性对齐；3）域对抗学习——减少视觉表示中的实体特定信息。

Result: 在动作预测和真实世界灵巧操作实验中取得一致改进：1）优于基线方法；2）实现零样本人类到机器人的技能迁移；3）泛化到未见过的物体和环境；4）支持数据高效的小样本适应。

Conclusion: AdvDex通过统一框架成功整合人类和机器人演示数据，利用规范动作表示和域对抗学习有效解决跨实体泛化问题，为灵巧操作的可扩展性提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation is a fundamental capability for embodied intelligence, but scaling it remains difficult because robot demonstrations are expensive to collect and action spaces vary across embodiments. Policies trained on heterogeneous data can also entangle task-relevant visual cues with embodiment-specific appearance, limiting cross-embodiment generalization. We present AdvDex, a unified Vision-Language-Action framework for learning dexterous manipulation from human and robot demonstrations. First, we introduce OmniShare, a large-scale multimodal dataset of human manipulation demonstrations that provides high-quality kinematic supervision and tactile measurements while reducing reliance on robot teleoperation. Second, we propose the Joint-Aligned Action Space (JAAS), a canonical action representation comprising an $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ wrist pose and 15 finger joints, thereby functionally aligning human hands, dexterous robot hands, and parallel grippers. Finally, we use domain-adversarial learning to reduce embodiment-specific information in the learned visual representation. Experiments on hand-action prediction and real-world dexterous manipulation show consistent improvements over baselines, effective zero-shot human-to-robot skill transfer, generalization to unseen objects and environments, and data-efficient few-shot adaptation.

</details>


### [124] [Demonstration of Space Robot Teleoperation over a Lossy and Delayed Network using ATMOS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14031)
*Inkyu Jang,Gregorio Marchesini,Nicola De Carli,Byeongjun Kim,Sunwoo Hwang,Dabin Kim,Elias Krantz,Youngkyoung Kong,Frank J. Jiang,Annika Wong,Pedro Roque,Prasetyo W. L. Sanjaya,Nicola Bastianello,Mani H. Dhullipalla,Karl H. Johansson,Hyungbo Shim,Dimos V. Dimarogonas,H. Jin Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ATMOS，一种用于模拟微重力环境下航天器遥操作控制的平面飞行器类比机器人，并在首尔与斯德哥尔摩之间进行了远程对接实验，验证了状态预测与轨迹跟踪结合的控制策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航天器遥操作面临通信延迟问题，需要在地面高效、低成本地验证微重力环境下的引导与控制策略。

Method: 开发ATMOS硬件在环测试平台，设计结合状态预测和轨迹跟踪的控制架构，进行跨洲远程实验，模拟随机时变通信延迟下的对接机动。

Result: 实验表明该控制策略能够有效处理通信延迟，实现远程对接；ATMOS平台支持快速、可靠、低成本的航天器遥操作概念测试。

Conclusion: ATMOS为微重力类环境下的在轨操作验证提供了第一步，展示了其在航天器遥操作概念快速可靠测试中的应用价值。

Abstract: We present a demonstration showcasing the Autonomy Testbed for Multi-purpose Orbiting Systems (ATMOS), a planar spacecraft-analog robot designed for hardware-in-the-loop evaluation of guidance and control strategies in microgravity-like conditions. Using ATMOS as the physical test platform, we investigate the design, analysis, and performance evaluation of control architectures for remotely operated spacecraft under round-trip communication delays. In this work, we develop and experimentally validate a control strategy that combines state prediction and trajectory tracking control to perform a docking maneuver, accounting for time-varying random communication latency between ground operators and the ATMOS system. The demonstration includes a long-distance remote control experiment between Seoul and Stockholm, introducing realistic intercontinental delays and variability. The results highlight the capability of ATMOS to support rapid, reliable, and cost-effective testing of spacecraft teleoperation concepts, establishing a first step toward robust validation of on-orbit operations in microgravity-like environments.

</details>


### [125] [Evolve Vision-Language-Action Model into an Agent with On-the-fly Tool-use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14047)
*Yi Ding,Yanzhao Yu,Xili Dai,Xianbiao Qi,Peiwen Sun,Xueqian Wang,Xiangyu Yue,Jianan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic Robot with Tool-use（ART），一个将视觉-语言-动作模型与工具使用集成的框架。该方法通过注入现成工具模块来增强底层视觉、高层可供性和具身能力，有效降低了动作解空间复杂度。在远小于基线的数据集上训练后，ART在仿真和真实世界任务中实现了20%的成功率提升，并展现出高效训练、轻量部署和可扩展的工具集成优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统端到端VLA模型面临连续动作解空间复杂度高、泛化性差、数据依赖性强等问题。作者旨在通过工具使用降低动作解空间复杂度，从而提升跨任务泛化能力并减少数据需求。

Method: 作者构建了一个包含3万条工具使用轨迹和行动演示的数据集，设计了面向挑战性环境的长轨迹工具使用推理训练方案，并开发了ART工具注入框架，通过微调任何VLA模型以利用现成的工具模块实现低层视觉、高层可供性和具身增强。

Result: ART在仿真和真实世界任务（如黑暗环境中新视角下的抓取放置）中取得了比主流基线方法高20%的成功率。实证结果表明，基于智能体的方法在训练效率、轻量级部署和新工具的可扩展集成方面具有显著优势，且所用数据集远小于基线方法。

Conclusion: 基于智能体的模块化工具使用方案能够显著提升视觉-语言-动作系统的鲁棒性、适应性和可扩展性，为复杂现实场景中的实际部署铺平道路。

Abstract: This paper integrates end-to-end Visual-Language-Action (VLA) models with agentic tool-use to propose Agentic Robot with Tool-use (ART). ART is a tool-injection framework that tunes any VLA model to leverage off-the-shelf tool modules for low-level vision, high-level affordance, and embodiment enhancement. Compared to vanilla VLA models with a whole continuous action solution space, ART reduces the complexity of the action solution space through tool-use, which not only improves generalizability across different tasks but also reduces data dependency. To demonstrate the advantages (high generalizability and low data dependency) of this framework, we first built a dataset of 30K tool-use trajectories and action demonstrations, which is much smaller than those used by baseline methods. We then designed a training regimen for long-trajectory tool-use reasoning in challenging environments. Experiments show that ART achieves a 20% higher success rate than mainstream baselines on simulation and real-world tasks, such as pick-and-place in the dark at novel viewpoints. Empirical results highlight the benefits of an agent-based approach: modular tool utilization enables more efficient training, lightweight deployment, and scalable integration of new tools. This design fosters robustness, adaptability, and extensibility, paving the way for the practical deployment of VLA systems in complex real-world scenarios.

</details>


### [126] [FlatLab: A Unified Methodology Framework and Simulation-Based Benchmark for Robotic Manipulation of Flat Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14049)
*Xingyu Zhu,Wenshuo Han,Zhouyu Wang,Yuran Wang,Ruihai Wu,Hao Dong,Fan Tang,Hechang Chen,Hyung Jin Chang,Yixing Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于机器人平面物体操作的统一框架，将策略生成与动作执行解耦，并引入了FlatLab仿真基准进行系统性评估。实验结果表明该方法能有效泛化到未见物体并超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面物体操作面临两大挑战：1)物体存在不可抓取的配置状态；2)物体几何形状和材料特性变化大。现有方法依赖启发式预操作，且在有限封闭环境中评估，泛化能力不足。

Method: 提出包含两个模块的统一框架：1)策略生成器：通过模拟数据变换和对比学习从物体点云中学习策略中心、物体不变的表示，预测合适的操作策略；2)动作执行模块：将长时序操作分解为可复用的动作原语，并动态组合生成稳定轨迹。同时构建了FlatLab仿真基准，提供高保真物理仿真、多样化刚性和柔性平面物体、自动化多模态数据收集及标准化任务定义和评估协议。

Result: 在FlatLab中进行的实验表明，该方法能够有效泛化到未见物体和类别，性能优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 该统一框架通过解耦策略生成与动作执行，解决了平面物体操作中的泛化问题；FlatLab基准为未来研究提供了标准化评估平台，推动了机器人平面物体操作领域的发展。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation of flat objects is challenging due to the ungraspable configurations and strong variations in object geometry and material. Existing methods rely on heuristic pre-manipulation and are often evaluated in closed settings with limited generalization. We propose a unified framework that decouples the manipulation into a strategy generator and an action execution module. The strategy generator predicts appropriate manipulation strategies from object point clouds by learning strategy-centric, object-invariant representations via simulated data transformation and contrastive learning. Conditioned on the predicted strategy, the execution module decomposes long-horizon manipulation into reusable action primitives and dynamically composes them to generate stable trajectories. To enable systematic evaluation, we introduce FlatLab, a comprehensive simulation benchmark for robotic flat object manipulation. FlatLab provides high-fidelity physical simulation of diverse rigid and deformable flat objects, automated multi-modal data collection, and standardized task definitions and evaluation protocols. Experiments conducted in FlatLab demonstrate that our approach generalizes effectively to unseen objects and categories, outperforming existing baselines. The project page and the code are provided at https://flatlab-web.github.io/.

</details>


### [127] [PILOT: Privileged Imitation Learning for End-to-End Motion Planning of Autonomous UAVs under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14082)
*Qingrui Zhang,Feng Xue,Xiang Zhou,Chenghao Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PILOT是一个基于视觉的端到端无人机运动规划框架，通过特权模仿学习将计算密集的最优控制策略蒸馏为轻量化策略，在部分可观测环境下实现自主导航。该框架通过时空感知融合模块增强空间感知，在计算开销降低80%以上的情况下达到与专家模型相当的性能，并成功实现室内外零样本部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在杂乱环境中的自主导航受到部分可观测性和动态约束的阻碍。传统方法在计算效率和实时性方面存在瓶颈，需要一种能够在有限观测条件下满足安全和动态约束的高效运动规划方法。

Method: 1. 约束感知特权模仿学习框架：将计算密集的最优控制专家策略蒸馏至学生策略网络
2. 双目标损失函数：同时正则化安全性和动态需求约束
3. 时空感知融合模块：使用时序卷积网络整合历史深度图像和里程计数据，推断任务相关的潜在上下文
4. 轨迹参数化层：将网络输出映射为结构化轨迹，在训练中引入连续性、动态一致性和障碍软惩罚机制
5. 无需持久地图记忆，增强即时视野外的空间感知能力

Result: 1. 在四旋翼和固定翼飞机仿真中，PILOT达到与特权专家相当的性能
2. 计算开销降低超过80%
3. 成功实现室内和室外零样本部署
4. 验证了规划器的实际应用可行性和跨域泛化能力

Conclusion: PILOT框架通过约束感知的特权模仿学习有效解决了部分可观测环境下的无人机运动规划问题。该框架在保证安全性和动态约束的前提下，显著降低了计算开销，实现了高效实时规划，并在零样本场景下展现出良好的跨域泛化能力，为实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in cluttered environments is hampered by partial observability and dynamic constraints. This paper presents PILOT, a constraint-aware privileged imitation learning framework for vision-based end-to-end UAV motion planning under partial observability. The framework distills planning strategies from a computationally intensive optimal control expert into a student policy regularized toward safety and dynamic requirements via a dual-objective loss function. To mitigate partial observability, a spatiotemporal perception fusion module using a Temporal Convolutional Network (TCN) is developed to integrate historical depth images and odometry. This module infers task-relevant latent context from historical observations, enhancing spatial awareness beyond the instantaneous FOV without maintaining persistent map memory. A trajectory parameterization layer mapping network outputs to a structured trajectory, while enabling explicit continuity, dynamic-consistency, and obstacle soft penalties during training, encouraging constraint satisfaction for unseen observations without formal guarantees. Simulations on quadrotor and fixed-wing aircraft demonstrate that PILOT achieves performance comparable to the privileged expert while reducing computational overhead by over 80\%. Successful indoor and outdoor zero-shot deployment confirms the practical feasibility and cross-domain generalization of the planner.

</details>


### [128] [AgilePE: Autonomous UAV Pursuit-Evasion via Self-Play Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14135)
*Wenhao Tang,Tianyang Chen,Zhejun Cui,Boyuan An,Jiayu Chen,Ruize Zhang,Huidong Liu,Tianyue Wu,Qingmin Liao,Fei Gao,Yu Wang,Chao Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AgilePE，一种基于自博弈强化学习的自主无人机追逐-规避系统，通过竞争策略优化和仿真到实机转移，实现无需任务特定调整的零样本部署，在真实四旋翼上复现了快速闪避和侧翼包抄等战术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主追逐-规避是无人机的基本挑战，需要在紧耦合动力学和对手行为持续变化的情况下进行快速决策。传统基于规则或微分博弈的方法难以处理高维空中交互和敏捷机动。

Method: AgilePE将敏捷底层控制、竞争策略优化和仿真到实机部署集成于统一框架。策略直接将机载状态观测映射到集体推力和机体速率（CTBR）指令，实现端到端敏捷机动，无需中间轨迹规划器或航点控制器。训练采用带优先虚拟自博弈（PFSP）的竞争性自博弈及多样化对手池，使智能体能够对抗历史策略进行改进，同时稳定优化并减少策略振荡。为实机部署，开发了硬件对齐的仿真管线，建模执行器响应动力学、通信延迟和域随机化。

Result: 学到的策略可零样本转移到真实四旋翼无人机，无需任务特定调整。真实世界实验复现了仿真中观察到的追逐-规避战术，包括快速闪避和侧翼包抄，并展示了交互式双智能体零样本部署。

Conclusion: 该过程导致复杂追逐和规避策略的涌现，系统成功实现零样本实机部署。

Abstract: Autonomous pursuit-evasion is a fundamental challenge for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs), requiring rapid decision-making under tightly coupled dynamics and continuously changing opponent behaviors. Traditional rule-based or differential-game approaches often struggle with high-dimensional aerial interactions and agile maneuvering. We present AgilePE, a complete system for autonomous UAV pursuit-evasion via self-play reinforcement learning. AgilePE integrates agile low-level control, competitive policy optimization, and sim-to-real deployment in a unified framework. The policy directly maps onboard state observations to Collective Thrust and Body Rates (CTBR) commands, enabling end-to-end agile maneuvering without intermediate trajectory planners or waypoint controllers. For training, we use competitive self-play with Prioritized Fictitious Self-Play (PFSP) and a diversified opponent pool, enabling agents to improve against historical policies while stabilizing optimization and reducing policy oscillation. This process leads to the emergence of sophisticated pursuit and evasion strategies. For real-world deployment, we develop a hardware-aligned simulation pipeline that models actuator-response dynamics, communication latency, and domain randomization. The learned policies transfer zero-shot to real quadrotors without task-specific tuning. Real-world experiments reproduce pursuit-evasion tactics observed in simulation, including rapid dodging and flanking, and demonstrate interactive two-agent zero-shot deployment.

</details>


### [129] [OccPlanner: Goal-Aware Occupancy-Conditioned Diffusion Planner for Pixel-Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14160)
*Binling Huang,Nianjin Ye,Xi Yang,Liang Hu,Zhou Huang,Shuang Wei,Longrui Yang,Yanchi Chen,Lanpeng Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出OccPlanner，一个目标感知的占用条件扩散规划器，通过在以自我为中心的度量空间中定位像素目标并利用3D占用特征，在5-8米距离设置中将平均成功率从20.81%提升至71.55%，展现出显著的导航性能提升和初步的仿真到现实迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素目标导航直接在智能体摄像头视图中指定目标，但目标像素既不提供度量深度也不提供可穿越性信息，使得3D目标定位和无碰撞连续规划面临挑战。

Method: 提出OccPlanner，将目标表示顺序条件于时间视觉上下文和学习的局部3D占用特征；引入L3ROcc，通过几何重建和基于射线的可见性推理将单目RGB导航视频转换为以机器人为中心的局部3D占用标注；在InternData-N1上训练，在InternScenes的四个未见场景类别和两个目标距离范围内进行闭环仿真评估。

Result: 在5-8米设置中，OccPlanner在四个类别中将平均成功率从NavDP的20.81%提升至71.55%，在杂乱-简单和杂乱-困难场景分别达到86.20%和84.92%；在Unitree Go2上的真实世界开环实验为使用L3ROcc生成监督的仿真到现实迁移和适应性提供了初步证据。

Conclusion: OccPlanner通过整合3D占用信息显著提升了像素目标导航性能，L3ROcc生成的占用监督实现了有效的仿真到现实迁移，为复杂环境中的自主导航提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Pixel-goal navigation specifies targets directly in the agent's camera view, but a target pixel provides neither metric depth nor traversability, making 3D goal grounding and collision-free continuous planning challenging. We present OccPlanner, a goal-aware occupancy-conditioned diffusion planner that grounds pixel goals in egocentric metric space and sequentially conditions the goal representation on temporal visual context and learned local 3D occupancy features. To provide occupancy supervision at scale, we introduce L3ROcc, which converts monocular RGB navigation videos into robot-centric local 3D occupancy annotations through geometric reconstruction and ray-based visibility reasoning. We train OccPlanner on InternData-N1 and evaluate it in closed-loop simulation across four unseen scene categories from InternScenes and two goal-distance ranges. In the 5-8 m setting, OccPlanner increases the average success rate (SR) over NavDP from 20.81% to 71.55% across the four categories, reaching 86.20% and 84.92% in cluttered-easy and cluttered-hard scenes, respectively. Real-world open-loop experiments on a Unitree Go2 further provide initial evidence of sim-to-real transfer and adaptation with L3ROcc-generated supervision.

</details>


### [130] [MMUSV-Sim: A Perception-Oriented Simulation and Data-Generation Platform for Multi-USV Cooperative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14207)
*Ziao Li,Jianxiong Ye,Biao Tang,Leping Zhang,Kun Zuo,Siyu Huang,Chenqiang Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MMUSV-Sim，一个基于虚幻引擎5和Project AirSim的海洋多无人艇合作感知仿真与数据生成平台，支持多种海洋环境、天气海况配置和多模态传感器数据采集，实验验证了多艇合作感知在目标检测任务上相比单艇的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多无人艇合作感知通过融合互补观测，能够超越单平台视野范围进行海洋目标感知。要大规模开发此类系统，需要一个统一的工作流来支持可配置的multi-USV场景、多模态采集和共享标注。

Method: 基于虚幻引擎5和Project AirSim构建MMUSV-Sim仿真平台，提供岛屿、开阔海域和港口环境，可配置天气、时段、海况条件，包含多样化船艇资产库，支持基于样条曲线的多艇运动规划。平台能够获取RGB、深度、语义、LiDAR和雷达多模态观测数据，并捕获共同世界状态用于每代理标注导出。

Result: 实验验证了配置的海况设置能够产生预期的船艇垂荡、横摇和纵摇变化，并评估了投影标注与语义渲染之间的几何一致性。在生成的多USV数据集上的LiDAR基合作BEV船艇检测实验中，早期融合方法达到72.74的AP@0.5，而单USV仅为45.54。

Conclusion: MMUSV-Sim平台成功验证了多无人艇合作感知的有效性，在船艇检测任务上通过多源数据融合显著提升了检测性能，为海洋合作感知系统开发提供了完整的仿真和数据生成解决方案。

Abstract: Cooperative perception among multiple unmanned surface vehicles (USVs) combines complementary observations to extend maritime target sensing beyond the view range and field of a single platform. Developing such systems at scale calls for a unified workflow for configurable multi-USV scenarios, multimodal acquisition, and shared annotations. We present MMUSV-Sim, a perception-oriented maritime simulation and data-generation platform built on Unreal Engine 5 and Project AirSim. It provides island, open-sea, and port environments; configurable weather, time of day, and wave conditions; a diverse vessel asset library; and spline-based multi-vessel motion. MMUSV-Sim acquires RGB, depth, semantic, LiDAR, and radar observations across multiple USVs and captures a common world state for per-agent annotation export. Experiments verify that the configured wave settings produce the intended changes in vessel heave, roll, and pitch, and evaluate the geometric consistency between projected annotations and semantic renderings. In LiDAR-based cooperative BEV vessel detection experiments on the generated multi-USV dataset, Early Fusion achieves an AP@0.5 of 72.74, compared with 45.54 using a single USV.

</details>


### [131] [Vibration Suppression in Collaborative Flexible Payload Manipulation Using Passive Force Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14244)
*Alaa Abderrahim,Antonio Rosales,Ferdinando Milella,Markku Suomalainen,Shuai Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种协作负载操控策略，采用双工业机器人主从构型来抑制大型柔性结构运动过程中的横向振动，特别适用于聚变反应堆等前沿应用的远程维护任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型重型结构在运动过程中产生显著振动，给精确操控带来重大挑战。在未来聚变能反应堆(托卡马克)等前沿项目中，远程维护此类结构的操控是关键任务，需要有效的振动抑制控制算法。

Method: 采用双工业机器人主从构型的协作负载操控方法：主机器人通过整形速度指令引导运动，从机器人通过导纳控制器确保对主机器人施加外力的顺应性。使用等效质量弹簧阻尼模型建立系统动力学，通过适当选择导纳参数实现系统能量被动耗散，并提供稳定性证明。

Result: 所提方法能够协作操控更重更大的柔性物体，解决了振动抑制和异构机器人规格等额外挑战。数值模拟验证了方法有效性，实验结果表明其实际效果。

Conclusion: 本文提出的协作负载操控策略能够有效抑制柔性负载运动过程中的横向振动，通过主从构型的双机器人系统实现了大型重型结构的精确操控，并提供了理论稳定性保证和实验验证。

Abstract: In large and heavy structures, vibrations arise during motion, posing significant challenges for precise manipulation. To accomplish the desired motion, control algorithms must effectively suppress these structural vibrations. In cutting edge projects, such as remote maintenance of future fusion energy reactors (tokamaks), the manipulation of this type of structure is defined as a crucial task. This paper presents a control strategy to suppress transverse vibrations in flexible payloads during motion using a collaborative payload manipulation approach. Two different industrial robot arms are arranged in a leader follower configuration for the manipulation strategy. The leader robot guides the motion with shaped velocity commands, while the follower robot ensures compliance with the estimated external forces applied by the leader on the payload through an admittance controller. Unlike existing methods, the proposed approach enables collaborative manipulation of heavier and larger flexible objects, addressing additional challenges such as vibration suppression and heterogeneous robot specifications. The dynamics of the leader follower payload system are modeled using an equivalent mass spring damper model, and it is shown that, with appropriate admittance parameters, the total energy of the system is passively dissipated. A stability proof is also provided. Numerical simulations validate the proposed method, and experimental results demonstrate its effectiveness.

</details>


### [132] [Accelerating Large-scale Bundle Adjustment for LiDAR Mapping via Parallel Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14266)
*Yixi Cai,Rundong Li,Yuhan Xie,Qingwen Zhang,Patric Jensfelt,Fu Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出首个完全并行计算框架，通过自适应数据加载、自底向上体素化和基于极大化-极小化 formulations 的并行优化算法，将大规模LiDAR bundle adjustment的计算机效率提升10倍，同时保持与最先进方法相当的建图精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR bundle adjustment广泛应用于构建全局一致的点云地图，但大规模场景下的计算效率较低，特别是在内存受限的GPU上处理大量点云数据时面临挑战。现有方法缺乏完全并行的计算框架来加速这一过程，亟需解决大规模建图中的计算瓶颈问题。

Method: 提出一种完全并行计算框架，包含三个关键技术：(1)自适应异步数据加载策略，高效处理内存受限GPU上的大规模点云数据集；(2)新颖的自底向上体素化方法提取平面特征，实现完全并行的预处理；(3)基于极大化-极小化公式的方法，通过并行计算加速残差、雅可比矩阵、Hessian矩阵计算及增量求解器等计算密集型任务。同时提供理论分析和时间复杂度证明。

Result: 在多个大规模公开数据集和不同计算平台上进行广泛基准测试，验证了方法的鲁棒性和适应性。实现了最高10倍的计算效率提升，同时保持了与最先进方法相当的建图精度。代码已在GitHub开源，便于后续研究。

Conclusion: 本文成功解决了LiDAR bundle adjustment在大规模建图中的计算效率问题，通过创新的并行计算框架显著提升了性能，为实际应用场景提供了高效解决方案，并将促进该领域后续研究发展。

Abstract: LiDAR bundle adjustment is widely utilized in mapping to construct globally consistent point cloud maps. In this paper, we propose the first fully parallel computing framework to accelerate LiDAR bundle adjustment for large-scale mapping, incorporating three key techniques. First, we design an adaptive, asynchronous data loading strategy to efficiently process large-scale point cloud datasets on memory-constrained GPUs. Secondly, we present a novel bottom-up voxelization method for extracting planar features, enabling fully parallelized pre-processing. Thirdly, we build upon a majorization-minimization formulation to accelerate compute-intensive tasks in the optimization via parallel computation, including the computation of residuals, Jacobian and Hessian matrices, and a parallel increment solver. To support our design, we provide both theoretical and experimental analysis of the time complexity of our approach. Extensive benchmarking on large-scale public datasets across various computational platforms validates the robustness and adaptability of our approach, achieving up to a tenfold improvement in computational efficiency while preserving mapping accuracy comparable to state-of-the-art methods. To benefit future research, the implementation code is available on GitHub.

</details>


### [133] [PRM-as-a-Judge 1.5: A Toolkit for Robot Process Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14284)
*Yuyang Liu,Yanqing Shen,Ruike Chen,Jifan Zhao,Yuxuan Tian,Yichi Zhang,Tianfeng Long,Zixuan Yin,Yipu Wang,Ziheng Qin,Wenxing Tan,Yang Shi,Mingyu Cao,Runze Xiao,Ziqi Wang,Zhixin Yin,Shiwei Chu,Yi-Fan Zhang,Yao Mu,Yuheng Ji,Yihao Wang,Jun Yan,Zhongyuan Wang,Pengwei Wang,Xiaolong Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PRM-as-a-Judge 1.5工具包，通过细粒度的机器人过程评估超越传统的二元成功率和规则化评分，引入三个新指标，发布RoboPulse++可靠性评估工具，并开源评估套件，呼吁社区建立透明、可复现的机器人评估标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人评估主要依赖二元成功率指标和基于规则的过程评分，这些方法无法充分理解具身模型的能力。需要开发更细粒度的评估方法，深入分析机器人在执行任务过程中的表现细节，以推动具身智能的进步。

Method: 1. 开发PRM-as-a-Judge 1.5工具包，将执行视频转换为密集进展曲线；2. 基于1.0版本引入三个新指标：失败侧进展、回撤后恢复能力、成功侧执行质量；3. 创建RoboPulse++评估流程奖励模型(PRM)的可靠性；4. 发布包含基准测试、指标实现和可视化工具的用户友好评估套件。

Result: 对基准测试中的具身模型进行了综合评估，提供了细粒度的指标结果和关键发现；建立了更准确的PRM测试平台；开源了支持可复现操作过程评估的完整工具集，包括基准测试、指标实现和可视化功能。

Conclusion: 呼吁机器人研究社区重新思考评估方法，建立透明、程序化、可复现的评估体系作为下一代具身智能的基础。该工作为机器人过程评估提供了完整的理论框架和实用工具，推动评估标准从粗粒度的成功/失败向细粒度的过程质量分析转变。

Abstract: Fine-grained robotic evaluation matters for understanding embodied models, going beyond binary success rates and rule-based process scores. We present PRM-as-a-Judge 1.5, a toolkit for robot process assessment that turns rollout videos into dense progress curves and derives multiple fine metrics. PRM-as-a-Judge 1.5 introduces three metrics, building on version 1.0, that characterize failure-side progress, post-drawdown recovery, and success-side execution quality, helping users understand embodied model capability. Based on the rollout videos from benchmarks, we perform a comprehensive assessment of the embodied models, providing some fine-grained metric results and key findings. We further introduce RoboPulse++ to evaluate the reliability of process reward models (PRM), providing evaluators with a more accurate testing platform. Moreover, we release a user-friendly assessment suite, including the benchmark, metric implementation, and visualization tools, to support reproducible manipulation process evaluation. We call on the community to rethink how robots are evaluated and establish transparent, procedural, and reproducible assessment as a foundation for the next generation of embodied intelligence.

</details>


### [134] [CORAL: Curriculum-Optimized Reward Adaptation for LiDAR-Based Goal-Directed Urban Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14332)
*Anisa Saleem,Duksu Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CORAL通过五阶段课程学习与阶段感知奖励机制结合，训练自动驾驶策略在复杂城市环境中同时掌握目标导航、路线跟踪、避障和遵守交通规则等多项竞争行为，在测试中达到100%成功率（基线仅5-10%），并能零样本迁移到未见过的城镇环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在自动驾驶中具有前景，但长视野目标导航需要策略同时掌握多项竞争行为（到达远距目标、跟踪路线、避障、遵守信号），固定目标函数无法提供学习这些行为的顺序安排。

Method: CORAL采用双进度机制：(1)五阶段课程逐步延长路线长度并收紧行为约束；(2)阶段感知奖励，其组件权重随任务难度提升，从关注任务进度转向路线跟踪、安全性、平滑性和规则遵守。策略采用多流Actor-Critic网络，使用PPO在CARLA上训练，输入为99维紧凑状态，包含极坐标激光雷达直方图、车辆遥测、自我坐标系路线几何和交通规则指示器——无需点云编码器或鸟瞰图栅格化。

Result: 在相同协议下与两个PPO基线对比：CORAL在所有20个评估片段中最长路线上达到100%目标到达率，基线仅为5%和10%。因子消融实验显示两个进度机制缺一不可：移除任一机制都会降低成功率和路线完成率，同时禁用两者则成功率降至55%。训练于单一城镇的策略可零样本迁移至7个未见城镇，在100-150米相同长度路线上成功率为68-98%，平均横向偏差小于0.35米。

Conclusion: 课程学习与阶段感知奖励的组合对于复杂多行为强化学习任务有效，能够显著提升自动驾驶策略的导航能力和环境泛化性能，为RL在自动驾驶中的应用提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is promising for autonomous urban driving, but long-horizon goal-directed navigation asks a policy to acquire several competing behaviors at once--reaching a distant goal, tracking a route, avoiding obstacles, obeying signals--and a fixed objective gives no order in which to learn them. This paper presents CORAL, which advances two schedules together: a five-stage curriculum that progressively lengthens routes and tightens behavioral constraints, and a stage-aware reward whose component weights shift emphasis from mission progress toward route following, safety, smoothness, and rule compliance as the task hardens. The policy is a multi-stream actor-critic network trained with Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) in CARLA on a compact 99-dimensional state pairing a polar LiDAR histogram with vehicle telemetry, ego-frame route geometry, and traffic-rule indicators--no point-cloud encoder, no bird's-eye-view rasterization. Against two PPO baselines under an identical protocol, CORAL reaches the goal in all twenty evaluation episodes on the longest routes under the full set of behavioral constraints, where the baselines reach 5% and 10%; a factorial ablation shows that neither schedule alone matches their combination: removing either lowers both success and route completion, and disabling both drops success to 55%. Trained in one town, the policy transfers zero-shot to seven unseen towns, succeeding in 68-98% of episodes on routes of the same 100-150 m length, with mean lateral deviation below 0.35 m.

</details>


### [135] [Reflex: Enabling Fast and Predictive Vision-Language-Action Models for Reaction-Critical Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14379)
*Yuxuan Chen,Wanruo Zhang,Xiao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对现有VLA模型在动态交互场景中评估不足的问题，提出了ReflexBench基准测试和ReflexVLA模型，显著提升了反应关键型操作的动态表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型基准主要评估静态操作任务的泛化能力，严重忽视了动态交互场景的评估需求。作者需要解决这一研究空白，为反应关键型操作提供更好的评估框架和模型解决方案。

Method: 1. 提出ReflexBench基准测试，包含六个动态任务，引入了将模拟器步进与机器人控制解耦的评估框架，支持同步和异步推理下的可配置延迟。2. 开发ReflexVLA高效VLA模型，通过潜在未来预测和多帧时间融合增强时间推理能力，通过批处理视觉编码和CUDA图重放降低部署延迟。

Result: 实验表明，ReflexVLA在动态操作性能上持续改进，同时在标准静态操作基准测试上保持竞争性准确性。真实世界实验进一步证明了其在实际部署条件下的有效性。

Conclusion: ReflexBench填补了反应关键型操作评估的研究空白，而ReflexVLA展示了在动态操作场景中VLA模型的潜力。该方法无需大规模机器人数据预训练即可实现高效性能，为实际机器人应用提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have recently achieved promising performance in robotic manipulation. However, existing benchmarks mainly evaluate generalization on static manipulation tasks and largely overlook dynamic interaction scenarios. To address this gap, we present ReflexBench, a benchmark for reaction-critical manipulation. ReflexBench contains six dynamic tasks and introduces an evaluation framework that decouples simulator stepping from robot control while supporting configurable latency under synchronous and asynchronous inference. Building upon ReflexBench, we propose ReflexVLA, an efficient VLA model designed for reaction-critical manipulation without large-scale robot-data pretraining. ReflexVLA enhances temporal reasoning through latent future prediction and multi-frame temporal fusion within the vision backbone, while reducing deployment latency through batched visual encoding and CUDA Graph replay. Experiments show that ReflexVLA consistently improves dynamic manipulation performance while maintaining competitive accuracy on standard static manipulation benchmarks, and real-world experiments further demonstrate its effectiveness under practical deployment conditions. Project website: https://reflexvla.github.io

</details>
